I had this... this nonstop energy, you know? I couldn't even sit through a whole meal or watch a soccer game on TV without jumping up. I just felt like I had to be doing something useful. I guess I just felt really motivated to turn things around after losing my job. It felt good to be so focused, honestly. I wasn't doing anything dangerous or risky, I promise. Just... really busy with all my projects. I hope that makes sense? I just want to make sure I'm giving you the kind of examples you're looking for.

Um, oh gosh... I actually feel really terrible now. I know I told you a few minutes ago that my sleep was totally normal, and I really, really wasn't trying to lie to you, I promise! I just...
